Here’s how to approach it:

1. **Parse the Introspection Response**:
   You need to parse the response JSON recursively to get all types, fields, and nested fields. Each type in the introspection schema can have fields that refer to other types or nested fields, requiring a recursive traversal to get the full structure.

2. **Create a Recursive Data Structure**:
   Represent the schema structure with a recursive data model that allows each field to contain a list of subfields. This will let you recreate the hierarchical view for selection.

3. **Build the Tree Structure from Parsed Data**:
   Once you have a parsed recursive structure, you can use it to dynamically populate a UI control like a `TreeView` with checkboxes, where each level can have subfields.

Here's a sample code outline that illustrates this approach:

### Step 1: Define Recursive Data Models

Define classes to represent the schema, fields, and types recursively.

```csharp
public class GraphQLType
{
    public string Kind { get; set; }
    public string Name { get; set; }
    public string Description { get; set; }
    public List<GraphQLField> Fields { get; set; } = new List<GraphQLField>();
}

public class GraphQLField
{
    public string Name { get; set; }
    public string Description { get; set; }
    public GraphQLType Type { get; set; }  // Recursive type reference
}
```

### Step 2: Parse the JSON Response Recursively

Use a recursive function to parse the schema data deeply. This example uses `Newtonsoft.Json`:

```csharp
public GraphQLType ParseGraphQLType(JToken typeToken)
{
    var type = new GraphQLType
    {
        Kind = typeToken["kind"]?.ToString(),
        Name = typeToken["name"]?.ToString(),
        Description = typeToken["description"]?.ToString()
    };

    if (typeToken["fields"] != null)
    {
        foreach (var fieldToken in typeToken["fields"])
        {
            var field = new GraphQLField
            {
                Name = fieldToken["name"]?.ToString(),
                Description = fieldToken["description"]?.ToString(),
                Type = ParseGraphQLType(fieldToken["type"]) // Recursively parse field types
            };
            type.Fields.Add(field);
        }
    }

    return type;
}
```

### Step 3: Initiate Parsing from the Root Schema Node

Use the introspection schema’s `types` array to parse each type:

```csharp
public List<GraphQLType> ParseSchema(JObject schemaData)
{
    var types = new List<GraphQLType>();

    var typesToken = schemaData["__schema"]?["types"];
    if (typesToken != null)
    {
        foreach (var typeToken in typesToken)
        {
            types.Add(ParseGraphQLType(typeToken));
        }
    }

    return types;
}
```

### Step 4: Build a Hierarchical UI

Once you have parsed the schema into a recursive structure, use it to populate a UI component like a `TreeView`.

#### Example with `TreeView` in WinForms:

```csharp
private void PopulateTreeView(TreeNode parentNode, GraphQLType type)
{
    foreach (var field in type.Fields)
    {
        var fieldNode = new TreeNode(field.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(fieldNode);

        if (field.Type != null && field.Type.Fields.Count > 0)
        {
            PopulateTreeView(fieldNode, field.Type);
        }
    }
}
```

To use this function:

```csharp
private void LoadSchemaIntoTreeView(JObject schemaData)
{
    var types = ParseSchema(schemaData);
    foreach (var type in types)
    {
        TreeNode typeNode = new TreeNode(type.Name);
        myTreeView.Nodes.Add(typeNode);

        PopulateTreeView(typeNode, type);
    }
}
```

### Step 5: Generate Query from Selected Fields

After users select fields from the `TreeView`, traverse the `TreeView` to build a GraphQL query.

```csharp
private string GenerateQueryFromSelection(TreeNode node)
{
    var query = new StringBuilder();
    query.Append(node.Text + " { ");

    foreach (TreeNode childNode in node.Nodes)
    {
        if (childNode.Checked) // Include only checked nodes
        {
            query.Append(GenerateQueryFromSelection(childNode));
        }
    }

    query.Append(" } ");
    return query.ToString();
}
```

Invoke this on the root node of the selected `TreeView` items:

```csharp
private string BuildGraphQLQuery(TreeView treeView)
{
    var query = new StringBuilder("{ ");
    foreach (TreeNode node in treeView.Nodes)
    {
        if (node.Checked)
        {
            query.Append(GenerateQueryFromSelection(node));
        }
    }
    query.Append(" }");
    return query.ToString();
}
```

### Summary

- **Recursive Parsing**: Parse the introspection schema recursively to handle all nested fields.
- **TreeView**: Populate a `TreeView` or similar UI element using the parsed data.
- **Query Generation**: Traverse selected nodes to generate a GraphQL query string.

This approach allows you to interactively select fields in a hierarchical view and dynamically construct a GraphQL query based on the user’s selections.

## ASSISTANT
Certainly! Here’s a complete example that puts everything together, with comments indicating where each part goes. This code assumes you are working with a WinForms application and using `Newtonsoft.Json` to parse the GraphQL introspection response. We'll create a `TreeView` that allows you to select fields, and then build a query based on those selections.

### Full Example Code

Here’s the complete code to build the `GraphQLService` class, parse the introspection schema, populate a `TreeView` in WinForms, and generate a query based on the selected fields.

#### Step 1: Define Recursive Data Models

These models represent the structure of the GraphQL schema, including types and fields.

```csharp
using Newtonsoft.Json.Linq;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace Logistixs.ServerManager.Application
{
    public class GraphQLType
    {
        public string Kind { get; set; }
        public string Name { get; set; }
        public string Description { get; set; }
        public List<GraphQLField> Fields { get; set; } = new List<GraphQLField>();
    }

    public class GraphQLField
    {
        public string Name { get; set; }
        public string Description { get; set; }
        public GraphQLType Type { get; set; }  // Recursive type reference
    }
}
```

#### Step 2: Define the GraphQLService Class

This class will:
1. Send an introspection query to get the schema.
2. Parse the response JSON into a list of `GraphQLType` objects.
3. Populate the `TreeView` with the parsed data.
4. Generate a query based on selected fields in the `TreeView`.

```csharp
using GraphQL.Client.Http;
using GraphQL.Client.Serializer.Newtonsoft;
using Newtonsoft.Json.Linq;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace Logistixs.ServerManager.Application
{
    public class GraphQLService
    {
        private readonly GraphQLHttpClient _client;

        public GraphQLService(string endpointUrl, string token)
        {
            _client = new GraphQLHttpClient(endpointUrl, new NewtonsoftJsonSerializer());
            _client.HttpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new System.Net.Http.Headers.AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token);
        }

        // Step 3: Send Introspection Query and Parse the Response
        public async Task<List<GraphQLType>> GetSchemaAsync()
        {
            var introspectionQuery = @"
                query IntrospectionQuery {
                    __schema {
                        types {
                            kind
                            name
                            description
                            fields(includeDeprecated: true) {
                                name
                                description
                                type {
                                    kind
                                    name
                                    ofType {
                                        kind
                                        name
                                        ofType {
                                            kind
                                            name
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }";

            var request = new GraphQLRequest { Query = introspectionQuery };
            var response = await _client.SendQueryAsync<JObject>(request);

            var schemaData = response.Data?["__schema"];
            if (schemaData == null)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine("Schema data is null.");
                return new List<GraphQLType>();
            }

            return ParseSchema(schemaData);
        }

        // Step 4: Recursive Parsing of the Schema JSON
        private List<GraphQLType> ParseSchema(JToken schemaData)
        {
            var types = new List<GraphQLType>();

            foreach (var typeToken in schemaData["types"])
            {
                types.Add(ParseGraphQLType(typeToken));
            }

            return types;
        }

        private GraphQLType ParseGraphQLType(JToken typeToken)
        {
            var type = new GraphQLType
            {
                Kind = typeToken["kind"]?.ToString(),
                Name = typeToken["name"]?.ToString(),
                Description = typeToken["description"]?.ToString()
            };

            if (typeToken["fields"] != null)
            {
                foreach (var fieldToken in typeToken["fields"])
                {
                    var field = new GraphQLField
                    {
                        Name = fieldToken["name"]?.ToString(),
                        Description = fieldToken["description"]?.ToString(),
                        Type = ParseGraphQLType(fieldToken["type"])
                    };
                    type.Fields.Add(field);
                }
            }

            return type;
        }

        // Step 5: Populate the TreeView with Parsed Data
        public void PopulateTreeView(TreeView treeView, List<GraphQLType> types)
        {
            treeView.Nodes.Clear();
            foreach (var type in types)
            {
                var typeNode = new TreeNode(type.Name);
                PopulateTreeView(typeNode, type);
                treeView.Nodes.Add(typeNode);
            }
        }

        private void PopulateTreeView(TreeNode parentNode, GraphQLType type)
        {
            foreach (var field in type.Fields)
            {
                var fieldNode = new TreeNode(field.Name);
                parentNode.Nodes.Add(fieldNode);

                if (field.Type != null && field.Type.Fields.Count > 0)
                {
                    PopulateTreeView(fieldNode, field.Type);
                }
            }
        }

        // Step 6: Generate Query from Selected TreeView Nodes
        public string BuildGraphQLQuery(TreeView treeView)
        {
            var query = new StringBuilder("{ ");
            foreach (TreeNode node in treeView.Nodes)
            {
                if (node.Checked)
                {
                    query.Append(GenerateQueryFromSelection(node));
                }
            }
            query.Append(" }");
            return query.ToString();
        }

        private string GenerateQueryFromSelection(TreeNode node)
        {
            var query = new StringBuilder();
            query.Append(node.Text + " { ");

            foreach (TreeNode childNode in node.Nodes)
            {
                if (childNode.Checked) // Include only checked nodes
                {
                    query.Append(GenerateQueryFromSelection(childNode));
                }
            }

            query.Append(" } ");
            return query.ToString();
        }
    }
}
```

#### Step 3: Set Up the UI in WinForms

1. Add a `TreeView` (e.g., `treeViewSchema`) and a `Button` (e.g., `buttonGenerateQuery`) to your form.
2. When the form loads, initialize the `GraphQLService` and populate the `TreeView`.

#### Step 4: Wire Up the Form Code

In your form's code-behind, initialize `GraphQLService`, populate the `TreeView`, and handle query generation.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace Logistixs.ServerManager.Application
{
    public partial class MainForm : Form
    {
        private GraphQLService _graphQLService;

        public MainForm()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _graphQLService = new GraphQLService("your_endpoint_url", "your_token");
        }

        private async void MainForm_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            var types = await _graphQLService.GetSchemaAsync();
            _graphQLService.PopulateTreeView(treeViewSchema, types);
        }

        private void buttonGenerateQuery_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            var query = _graphQLService.BuildGraphQLQuery(treeViewSchema);
            MessageBox.Show("Generated Query:\n" + query);
        }
    }
}
```

### Explanation of Key Parts

1. **`GraphQLService.GetSchemaAsync()`**: Sends the introspection query and parses the response JSON into a list of `GraphQLType` objects.
2. **`GraphQLService.PopulateTreeView()`**: Populates the `TreeView` with a hierarchical structure representing the schema.
3. **`GraphQLService.BuildGraphQLQuery()`**: Generates a GraphQL query based on the checked nodes in the `TreeView`.

### Running the Code

1. Run your application, and when the form loads, the `TreeView` will display the parsed schema structure.
2. Check the boxes for fields you want to include in your query.
3. Click the "Generate Query" button, and a message box will show the dynamically generated GraphQL query.

This complete example provides a fully functional setup for parsing a GraphQL schema, displaying it in a `TreeView`, and generating a query based on selected fields.

To keep the TreeView logic separate and provide JSON that can be attached directly, let's modify the `GraphQLService` class to return a structured JSON that includes only the necessary introspection data, ready to be processed in the TreeView layer. This JSON output can capture the introspective structure, simplifying the TreeView setup.

Here's an updated `GraphQLService` class that returns JSON with a nested structure for easier parsing:

```csharp
using GraphQL.Client.Http;
using GraphQL.Client.Serializer.Newtonsoft;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Logistixs.ServerManager.Application
{
    public class GraphQLService
    {
        private readonly GraphQLHttpClient _client;

        public GraphQLService(string endpointUrl, string token)
        {
            _client = new GraphQLHttpClient(endpointUrl, new NewtonsoftJsonSerializer());
            _client.HttpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new System.Net.Http.Headers.AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token);
        }

        public async Task<string> GetSchemaJsonAsync()
        {
            var introspectionQuery = @"
                query IntrospectionQuery {
                    __schema {
                        types {
                            kind
                            name
                            fields {
                                name
                                type {
                                    kind
                                    name
                                    ofType {
                                        kind
                                        name
                                    }
                                }
                                args {
                                    name
                                    type {
                                        kind
                                        name
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            inputFields {
                                name
                                type {
                                    kind
                                    name
                                    ofType {
                                        kind
                                        name
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }";

            var request = new GraphQLRequest { Query = introspectionQuery };
            var response = await _client.SendQueryAsync<SchemaResponse>(request);

            // If we get a valid response, serialize the nested structure for easy use in the TreeView layer
            if (response.Data?.Schema?.Types != null)
            {
                var schemaJson = JsonConvert.SerializeObject(response.Data.Schema.Types, Formatting.Indented);
                Debug.WriteLine("Introspection JSON Schema:");
                Debug.WriteLine(schemaJson);
                return schemaJson;
            }
            else
            {
                Debug.WriteLine("Failed to retrieve schema.");
                return null;
            }
        }
    }

    // Define schema-related classes for introspection result deserialization
    public class SchemaResponse
    {
        [JsonProperty("__schema")]
        public Schema Schema { get; set; }
    }

    public class Schema
    {
        [JsonProperty("types")]
        public List<TypeElement> Types { get; set; }
    }

    public class TypeElement
    {
        [JsonProperty("kind")]
        public string Kind { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("fields")]
        public List<Field> Fields { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("inputFields")]
        public List<InputField> InputFields { get; set; }
    }

    public class Field
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("type")]
        public FieldType Type { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("args")]
        public List<Argument> Args { get; set; }
    }

    public class FieldType
    {
        [JsonProperty("kind")]
        public string Kind { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("ofType")]
        public FieldType OfType { get; set; }
    }

    public class Argument
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("type")]
        public FieldType Type { get; set; }
    }

    public class InputField
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("type")]
        public FieldType Type { get; set; }
    }
}
```

### Explanation

1. **GraphQLService Modifications**:
   - The `GetSchemaJsonAsync` method returns the introspection data as a JSON string.
   - This JSON can be directly consumed in the TreeView layer or any UI components needing a JSON format.

2. **Simplified JSON Structure**:
   - The JSON string includes `types`, `fields`, `inputFields`, and `args`, allowing the TreeView layer to parse and display the data in a nested structure.

3. **Output**:
   - The output JSON structure is organized for easy attachment to a UI layer, allowing further customization without embedding TreeView-specific logic in the `GraphQLService` class.

This approach lets the TreeView layer parse and manage the JSON, maintaining separation of concerns and allowing reuse of this introspective JSON data in other components if needed. You can now use this JSON directly in your TreeView or similar component for checkbox selection and hierarchical display.

lets rebuilt this to work with our new data using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Telerik.WinControls.UI;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using Logistixs.ServerManager.Application;

public class RadTreeViewPopulator
{
    private RadTreeView _treeView;

    public RadTreeViewPopulator(RadTreeView treeView)
    {
        _treeView = treeView;
    }

    public void PopulateTreeView(string jsonSchema)
    {
        // Deserialize the JSON into the Welcome class
       //Welcome welcomeData = Welcome.FromJson(jsonSchema);

        // Create root node
        var rootNode = new RadTreeNode("GraphQL Schema");
        _treeView.Nodes.Add(rootNode);

        // Add query, mutation, and types nodes
       // AddSchemaNodes(rootNode, welcomeData.Schema);

        // Expand all nodes for better visibility
        _treeView.ExpandAll();
    }

    private void AddSchemaNodes(RadTreeNode rootNode, Schema schema)
    {
        if (schema.QueryType != null)
        {
            var queryNode = new RadTreeNode($"Query Type: {schema.QueryType.Name}");
            rootNode.Nodes.Add(queryNode);
        }

        if (schema.MutationType != null)
        {
            var mutationNode = new RadTreeNode($"Mutation Type: {schema.MutationType.Name}");
            rootNode.Nodes.Add(mutationNode);
        }

        var typesNode = new RadTreeNode("Types");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(typesNode);

        foreach (var typeElement in schema.Types)
        {
            AddTypeNode(typesNode, typeElement);
        }

        var directivesNode = new RadTreeNode("Directives");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(directivesNode);

        foreach (var directive in schema.Directives)
        {
            AddDirectiveNode(directivesNode, directive);
        }
    }

    private void AddTypeNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, TypeElement typeElement)
    {
        var typeNode = new RadTreeNode($"{typeElement.Name} ({typeElement.Kind})");
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(typeNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(typeElement.Description))
        {
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {typeElement.Description}"));
        }

        if (typeElement.Fields != null)
        {
            var fieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(fieldsNode);
            foreach (var field in typeElement.Fields)
            {
                AddFieldNode(fieldsNode, field);
            }
        }

        if (typeElement.InputFields != null)
        {
            var inputFieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Input Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(inputFieldsNode);
            foreach (var inputField in typeElement.InputFields)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(inputFieldsNode, inputField);
            }
        }

        if (typeElement.EnumValues != null)
        {
            var enumValuesNode = new RadTreeNode("Enum Values");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(enumValuesNode);
            foreach (var enumValue in typeElement.EnumValues)
            {
                var enumNode = new RadTreeNode($"{enumValue.Name} - {enumValue.Description}");
                enumValuesNode.Nodes.Add(enumNode);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddFieldNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Field field)
    {
        var fieldNode = new RadTreeNode(field.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(fieldNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(field.Description))
        {
            fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {field.Description}"));
        }

        if (field.Args != null)
        {
            var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
            fieldNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
            foreach (var arg in field.Args)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
            }
        }

        fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(field.Type)}"));
    }

    private void AddArgumentNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Arg arg)
    {
        var argNode = new RadTreeNode(arg.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(argNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(arg.Description))
        {
            argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {arg.Description}"));
        }

        if (arg.Type != null)
        {
            argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(arg.Type)}"));
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(arg.DefaultValue))
        {
            argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Default Value: {arg.DefaultValue}"));
        }
    }

    private void AddDirectiveNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Directive directive)
    {
        var directiveNode = new RadTreeNode(directive.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(directiveNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(directive.Description))
        {
            directiveNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {directive.Description}"));
        }

        if (directive.Locations != null)
        {
            var locationsNode = new RadTreeNode("Locations");
            directiveNode.Nodes.Add(locationsNode);
            foreach (var location in directive.Locations)
            {
                locationsNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode(location));
            }
        }

        if (directive.Args != null)
        {
            var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
            directiveNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
            foreach (var arg in directive.Args)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
            }
        }
    }

    private string GetTypeDescription(OfTypeClass type)
    {
        if (type == null) return "Unknown";
        return type.OfType != null ? $"{type.Kind} of {GetTypeDescription(type.OfType)}" : type.Name;
    }
}

### `GraphQLService.cs`

This service class is designed to fetch the GraphQL schema and return it as a JSON string that will be used by the TreeView layer.

```csharp
using GraphQL.Client.Http;
using GraphQL.Client.Serializer.Newtonsoft;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Logistixs.ServerManager.Application
{
    public class GraphQLService
    {
        private readonly GraphQLHttpClient _client;

        public GraphQLService(string endpointUrl, string token)
        {
            _client = new GraphQLHttpClient(endpointUrl, new NewtonsoftJsonSerializer());
            _client.HttpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new System.Net.Http.Headers.AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token);
        }

        public async Task<string> GetSchemaJsonAsync()
        {
            var introspectionQuery = @"
                query IntrospectionQuery {
                    __schema {
                        types {
                            kind
                            name
                            fields {
                                name
                                type {
                                    kind
                                    name
                                    ofType {
                                        kind
                                        name
                                    }

                                args {

                                    type {
                                        kind
                                        name
                                    }

                                type {
                                    kind
                                    name
                                    ofType {
                                        kind
                                        name
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }";

            var request = new GraphQLRequest { Query = introspectionQuery };
            var response = await _client.SendQueryAsync<SchemaResponse>(request);

### `RadTreeViewPopulator.cs`

This `RadTreeViewPopulator` class uses the structured JSON returned by `GraphQLService` to populate the TreeView. It deserializes the JSON into `TypeElement` objects and builds nodes recursively.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Telerik.WinControls.UI;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Logistixs.ServerManager.Application;

public class RadTreeViewPopulator
{
    private RadTreeView _treeView;

    public RadTreeViewPopulator(RadTreeView treeView)
    {
        _treeView = treeView;
    }

    public void PopulateTreeView(string jsonSchema)
    {
        var types = JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<List<TypeElement>>(jsonSchema);

        // Create root node
        var rootNode = new RadTreeNode("GraphQL Schema");
        _treeView.Nodes.Add(rootNode);

        // Add types nodes
        foreach (var typeElement in types)
        {
            AddTypeNode(rootNode, typeElement);
        }

        // Expand all nodes for better visibility
        _treeView.ExpandAll();
    }

    private void AddTypeNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, TypeElement typeElement)
    {
        var typeNode = new RadTreeNode($"{typeElement.Name} ({typeElement.Kind})");
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(typeNode);

        if (typeElement.Fields != null)
        {
            var fieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(fieldsNode);
            foreach (var field in typeElement.Fields)
            {
                AddFieldNode(fieldsNode, field);
            }
        }

        if (typeElement.InputFields != null)
        {
            var inputFieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Input Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(inputFieldsNode);
            foreach (var inputField in typeElement.InputFields)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(inputFieldsNode, inputField.Name, inputField.Type);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddFieldNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Field field)
    {
        var fieldNode = new RadTreeNode(field.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(fieldNode);

        if (field.Args != null)
        {
            var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
            fieldNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
            foreach (var arg in field.Args)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg.Name, arg.Type);
            }
        }

        fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(field.Type)}"));
    }

    private void AddArgumentNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, string name, FieldType type)
    {
        var argNode = new RadTreeNode(name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(argNode);
        argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(type)}"));
    }

    private string GetTypeDescription(FieldType type)
    {
        if (type == null) return "Unknown";
        return type.OfType != null ? $"{type.Kind} of {GetTypeDescription(type.OfType)}" : type.Name;
    }
}
```

### Usage

After initializing the `GraphQLService`, you can use the `RadTreeViewPopulator` to load the TreeView as follows:

```csharp
var treeView = new RadTreeView();
var populator = new RadTreeViewPopulator(treeView);

var service = new GraphQLService("https://your-endpoint.com/graphql", "your-token");
var jsonSchema = await service.GetSchemaJsonAsync();

if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(jsonSchema))
{
    populator.PopulateTreeView(jsonSchema);
}
else
{
    MessageBox.Show("Failed to retrieve schema.");
}
```

### Explanation

- **GraphQLService**: Retrieves the schema data in JSON format, structured with fields, input fields, and arguments.
- **RadTreeViewPopulator**: Uses the JSON data to build a `RadTreeView` structure, with clear separation between the TreeView display logic and data retrieval.
- **PopulateTreeView**: Parses the JSON and builds the tree nodes based on the introspected schema.

This design keeps the responsibilities separate, making the service and UI layers modular and easily adaptable.

### Updated `GraphQLService.cs`

This service class will return the schema as JSON, but we’ll ensure the format is designed so that it’s easily parsed into a structure with `queries`, `mutations`, `subscriptions`, `directives`, and `types` as distinct categories.

```csharp
using GraphQL.Client.Http;
using GraphQL.Client.Serializer.Newtonsoft;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace Logistixs.ServerManager.Application
{
    public class GraphQLService
    {
        private readonly GraphQLHttpClient _client;

        public GraphQLService(string endpointUrl, string token)
        {
            _client = new GraphQLHttpClient(endpointUrl, new NewtonsoftJsonSerializer());
            _client.HttpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new System.Net.Http.Headers.AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token);
        }

        public async Task<string> GetSchemaJsonAsync()
        {
            var introspectionQuery = @"
                query IntrospectionQuery {
                    __schema {
                        queryType { name }
                        mutationType { name }
                        subscriptionType { name }
                        types {
                            kind
                            name
                            description
                            fields(includeDeprecated: true) {
                                name
                                description
                                args {
                                    name
                                    description
                                    type { kind name ofType { kind name ofType { kind name } } }
                                    defaultValue
                                }
                                type { kind name ofType { kind name ofType { kind name } } }
                            }
                            inputFields {
                                name
                                description
                                type { kind name ofType { kind name ofType { kind name } } }
                                defaultValue
                            }
                            interfaces { name }
                            enumValues(includeDeprecated: true) {
                                name
                                description
                            }
                            possibleTypes { name }
                        }
                        directives {
                            name
                            description
                            locations
                            args {
                                name
                                description
                                type { kind name ofType { kind name } }
                                defaultValue
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }";

            var request = new GraphQLRequest { Query = introspectionQuery };
            var response = await _client.SendQueryAsync<SchemaResponse>(request);

            if (response.Data?.Schema != null)
            {
                var schemaJson = JsonConvert.SerializeObject(response.Data.Schema, Formatting.Indented);
                Debug.WriteLine("Introspection JSON Schema:");
                Debug.WriteLine(schemaJson);
                return schemaJson;
            }
            else
            {
                Debug.WriteLine("Failed to retrieve schema.");
                return null;
            }
        }
    }

    public class SchemaResponse
    {
        [JsonProperty("__schema")]
        public Schema Schema { get; set; }
    }

    public class Schema
    {
        [JsonProperty("queryType")]
        public TypeReference QueryType { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("mutationType")]
        public TypeReference MutationType { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("subscriptionType")]
        public TypeReference SubscriptionType { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("types")]
        public List<TypeElement> Types { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("directives")]
        public List<Directive> Directives { get; set; }
    }

    public class TypeReference
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }
    }

    public class TypeElement
    {
        [JsonProperty("kind")]
        public string Kind { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("fields")]
        public List<Field> Fields { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("inputFields")]
        public List<InputField> InputFields { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("interfaces")]
        public List<TypeReference> Interfaces { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("enumValues")]
        public List<EnumValue> EnumValues { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("possibleTypes")]
        public List<TypeReference> PossibleTypes { get; set; }
    }

    public class Field
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("args")]
        public List<Argument> Args { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("type")]
        public FieldType Type { get; set; }
    }

    public class FieldType
    {
        [JsonProperty("kind")]
        public string Kind { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("ofType")]
        public FieldType OfType { get; set; }
    }

    public class Argument
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("type")]
        public FieldType Type { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("defaultValue")]
        public string DefaultValue { get; set; }
    }

    public class InputField
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("type")]
        public FieldType Type { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("defaultValue")]
        public string DefaultValue { get; set; }
    }

    public class EnumValue
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }
    }

    public class Directive
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("locations")]
        public List<string> Locations { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("args")]
        public List<Argument> Args { get; set; }
    }
}

### Updated `RadTreeViewPopulator.cs`

This `RadTreeViewPopulator` class will create the top-level nodes (`Queries`, `Mutations`, `Subscriptions`, `Directives`, and `Types`) and then recursively add the nested fields and arguments.

```csharp
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Telerik.WinControls.UI;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Logistixs.ServerManager.Application;

public class RadTreeViewPopulator
{
    private RadTreeView _treeView;

    public RadTreeViewPopulator(RadTreeView treeView)
    {
        _treeView = treeView;
    }

    public void PopulateTreeView(string jsonSchema)
    {
        var schema = JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<Schema>(jsonSchema);

        var rootNode = new RadTreeNode("GraphQL Schema");
        _treeView.Nodes.Add(rootNode);

        if (schema.QueryType != null)
            rootNode.Nodes.Add(CreateNode(schema.QueryType, "Queries", schema.Types));

        if (schema.MutationType != null)
            rootNode.Nodes.Add(CreateNode(schema.MutationType, "Mutations", schema.Types));

        if (schema.SubscriptionType != null)
            rootNode.Nodes.Add(CreateNode(schema.SubscriptionType, "Subscriptions", schema.Types));

        var directivesNode = new RadTreeNode("Directives");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(directivesNode);
        foreach (var directive in schema.Directives)
        {
            AddDirectiveNode(directivesNode, directive);
        }

        var typesNode = new RadTreeNode("Types");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(typesNode);
        foreach (var typeElement in schema.Types)
        {
            if (typeElement.Kind != "OBJECT" && typeElement.Kind != "INTERFACE") // Avoid duplicating query/mutation types
                AddTypeNode(typesNode, typeElement);
        }

        _treeView.ExpandAll();
    }

    private RadTreeNode CreateNode(TypeReference typeReference, string name, List<TypeElement> types)
    {
        var node = new RadTreeNode(name);
        var mainType = types.Find(t => t.Name == typeReference.Name);

        if (mainType?.Fields != null)
        {
            foreach (var field in mainType.Fields)
            {
                AddFieldNode(node, field);
            }
        }

        return node;
    }

    private void AddFieldNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Field field)
    {
        var fieldNode = new RadTreeNode(field.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(fieldNode);

        if (field.Args != null)
        {
            var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
            fieldNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
            foreach (var arg in field.Args)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
            }
        }

        fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(field.Type)}"));
    }

    private void AddArgumentNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Argument arg)
    {
        var argNode = new RadTreeNode(arg.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(argNode);
        argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(arg.Type)}"));
    }

### Updated `RadTreeViewPopulator.cs`

This class uses the unmodified introspection data returned from the `GraphQLService`. It organizes the `RadTreeView` into the specified top-level nodes and recursively builds each category.

```csharp
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Telerik.WinControls.UI;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using Logistixs.ServerManager.Application;

public class RadTreeViewPopulator
{
    private RadTreeView _treeView;

    public RadTreeViewPopulator(RadTreeView treeView)
    {
        _treeView = treeView;
    }

    public void PopulateTreeView(string jsonSchema)
    {
        var schema = JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<Schema>(jsonSchema);

        var rootNode = new RadTreeNode("GraphQL Schema");
        _treeView.Nodes.Add(rootNode);

        // Add Queries, Mutations, Subscriptions, Directives, and Types
        AddQueryNode(rootNode, "Queries", schema.QueryType, schema.Types);
        AddQueryNode(rootNode, "Mutations", schema.MutationType, schema.Types);
        AddQueryNode(rootNode, "Subscriptions", schema.SubscriptionType, schema.Types);
        AddDirectivesNode(rootNode, schema.Directives);
        AddTypesNode(rootNode, schema.Types);

        _treeView.ExpandAll();
    }

    private void AddQueryNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, string name, TypeReference typeReference, List<TypeElement> types)
    {
        if (typeReference != null)
        {
            var node = new RadTreeNode(name);
            rootNode.Nodes.Add(node);

            var mainType = types.Find(t => t.Name == typeReference.Name && t.Kind == "OBJECT");
            if (mainType?.Fields != null)
            {
                foreach (var field in mainType.Fields)
                {
                    AddFieldNode(node, field);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddDirectivesNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, List<Directive> directives)
    {
        var directivesNode = new RadTreeNode("Directives");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(directivesNode);

        foreach (var directive in directives)
        {
            var directiveNode = new RadTreeNode(directive.Name);
            directivesNode.Nodes.Add(directiveNode);

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(directive.Description))
            {
                directiveNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {directive.Description}"));
            }

            var locationsNode = new RadTreeNode("Locations");
            directiveNode.Nodes.Add(locationsNode);
            foreach (var location in directive.Locations)
            {
                locationsNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode(location));
            }

            var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
            directiveNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
            foreach (var arg in directive.Args)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddTypesNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, List<TypeElement> types)
    {
        var typesNode = new RadTreeNode("Types");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(typesNode);

        foreach (var typeElement in types)
        {
            // Filter out root query, mutation, and subscription types to avoid duplication
            if (typeElement.Kind != "OBJECT" || (typeElement.Name != "Query" && typeElement.Name != "Mutation" && typeElement.Name != "Subscription"))
            {
                AddTypeNode(typesNode, typeElement);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddTypeNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, TypeElement typeElement)
    {
        var typeNode = new RadTreeNode($"{typeElement.Name} ({typeElement.Kind})");
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(typeNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(typeElement.Description))
        {
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {typeElement.Description}"));
        }

        if (typeElement.Fields != null)
        {
            var fieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(fieldsNode);
            foreach (var field in typeElement.Fields)
            {
                AddFieldNode(fieldsNode, field);
            }
        }

        if (typeElement.InputFields != null)
        {
            var inputFieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Input Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(inputFieldsNode);
            foreach (var inputField in typeElement.InputFields)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(inputFieldsNode, inputField);
            }
        }

        if (typeElement.EnumValues != null)
        {
            var enumValuesNode = new RadTreeNode("Enum Values");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(enumValuesNode);
            foreach (var enumValue in typeElement.EnumValues)
            {
                var enumNode = new RadTreeNode($"{enumValue.Name} - {enumValue.Description}");
                enumValuesNode.Nodes.Add(enumNode);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddFieldNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Field field)
    {
        var fieldNode = new RadTreeNode(field.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(fieldNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(field.Description))
        {
            fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {field.Description}"));
        }

        if (field.Args != null && field.Args.Count > 0)
        {
            var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
            fieldNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
            foreach (var arg in field.Args)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
            }
        }

        fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(field.Type)}"));
    }

    private void AddArgumentNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Argument arg)
    {
        var argNode = new RadTreeNode(arg.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(argNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(arg.Description))
        {
            argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {arg.Description}"));
        }

        if (arg.Type != null)
        {
            argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(arg.Type)}"));
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(arg.DefaultValue))
        {
            argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Default Value: {arg.DefaultValue}"));
        }
    }

    private string GetTypeDescription(FieldType type)
    {
        if (type == null) return "Unknown";
        return type.OfType != null ? $"{type.Kind} of {GetTypeDescription(type.OfType)}" : type.Name;
    }
}
```

### Explanation

1. **Introspection Query**: The original query is fully preserved.
2. **PopulateTreeView**: Creates nodes for `Queries`, `Mutations`, `Subscriptions`, `Directives`, and `Types`.
3. **AddQueryNode**: Adds nodes specifically for queries, mutations, or subscriptions.
4. **AddDirectivesNode**: Processes the `directives` list and adds each directive along with its arguments and locations.
5. **AddTypesNode**: Adds other types (excluding query/mutation types) under the `Types` node.
6. **Recursion**: The code uses recursion to handle nested types, arguments, and fields to ensure each level is represented accurately.

This should give you the structure you’re aiming for, with the original introspection query intact and each top-level node categorized as requested. Let me know if this approach works better for you!

```csharp
using System;

namespace Logistixs.ServerManager.Application
{
    public class RadTreeViewPopulator
    {
        private RadTreeView _treeView;

        public RadTreeViewPopulator(RadTreeView treeView)
        {
            _treeView = treeView;
        }

        public void PopulateTreeView(string jsonSchema)
        {
            // Deserialize the JSON into the main schema class
            var schema = JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<Schema>(jsonSchema);

            var rootNode = new RadTreeNode("GraphQL Schema");
            _treeView.Nodes.Add(rootNode);

            // Add nodes for Queries, Mutations, Subscriptions, Directives, and Types
            if (schema.QueryType != null)
                AddOperationNode(rootNode, "Queries", schema.QueryType.Name, schema.Types);

            if (schema.MutationType != null)
                AddOperationNode(rootNode, "Mutations", schema.MutationType.Name, schema.Types);

            if (schema.SubscriptionType != null)
                AddOperationNode(rootNode, "Subscriptions", schema.SubscriptionType.Name, schema.Types);

            if (schema.Directives != null)
                AddDirectivesNode(rootNode, schema.Directives);

            if (schema.Types != null)
                AddTypesNode(rootNode, schema.Types);

            _treeView.ExpandAll();

        private void AddOperationNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, string operationName, string typeName, List<TypeElement> types)
        {
            var operationNode = new RadTreeNode(operationName);
            rootNode.Nodes.Add(operationNode);

            var mainType = types.Find(t => t.Name == typeName && t.Kind == "OBJECT");
            if (mainType?.Fields != null)
            {
                foreach (var field in mainType.Fields)
                {
                    AddFieldNode(operationNode, field);
                }
            }
        }

        private void AddDirectivesNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, List<Directive> directives)

            rootNode.Nodes.Add(directivesNode);

                directiveNode.Nodes.Add(locationsNode);

                {
                    locationsNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode(location));
                }

                var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
                directiveNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
                foreach (var arg in directive.Args)
                {
                    AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
                }
            }
        }

        private void AddTypesNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, List<TypeElement> types)
        {
            var typesNode = new RadTreeNode("Types");
            rootNode.Nodes.Add(typesNode);

            foreach (var typeElement in types)

                // Filter out main Query, Mutation, and Subscription types to avoid duplication
                if (typeElement.Kind != "OBJECT" ||

        private void AddTypeNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, TypeElement typeElement)
        {
            var typeNode = new RadTreeNode($"{typeElement.Name} ({typeElement.Kind})");
            parentNode.Nodes.Add(typeNode);

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(typeElement.Description))
                typeNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {typeElement.Description}"));

            if (typeElement.Fields != null)
            {
                var fieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Fields");
                typeNode.Nodes.Add(fieldsNode);
                foreach (var field in typeElement.Fields)
                {
                    AddFieldNode(fieldsNode, field);
                }
            }

            if (typeElement.InputFields != null)
            {
                var inputFieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Input Fields");
                typeNode.Nodes.Add(inputFieldsNode);
                foreach (var inputField in typeElement.InputFields)
                {
                    AddArgumentNode(inputFieldsNode, inputField);
                }
            }

            if (typeElement.EnumValues != null)
            {
                var enumValuesNode = new RadTreeNode("Enum Values");
                typeNode.Nodes.Add(enumValuesNode);
                foreach (var enumValue in typeElement.EnumValues)
                {
                    var enumNode = new RadTreeNode($"{enumValue.Name} - {enumValue.Description}");
                    enumValuesNode.Nodes.Add(enumNode);
                }
            }
        }

        private void AddFieldNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Field field)
        {
            var fieldNode = new RadTreeNode(field.Name);
            parentNode.Nodes.Add(fieldNode);

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(field.Description))
                fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {field.Description}"));

            if (field.Args != null && field.Args.Count > 0)
            {
                var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
                fieldNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
                foreach (var arg in field.Args)
                {
                    AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
                }
            }

            fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(field.Type)}"));
        }

        private void AddArgumentNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Argument arg)
        {
            var argNode = new RadTreeNode(arg.Name);
            parentNode.Nodes.Add(argNode);

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(arg.Description))
                argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {arg.Description}"));

            if (arg.Type != null)
                argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {GetTypeDescription(arg.Type)}"));

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(arg.DefaultValue))
                argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Default Value: {arg.DefaultValue}"));
        }

        private string GetTypeDescription(FieldType type)
        {
            if (type == null) return "Unknown";
            return type.OfType != null ? $"{type.Kind} of {GetTypeDescription(type.OfType)}" : type.Name;
        }
    }
}
```

### Explanation of Key Changes:

1. **PopulateTreeView**: Initializes the top-level nodes (`Queries`, `Mutations`, etc.) based on the schema and the type elements. Each top node is organized by calling specific helper methods.

2. **AddOperationNode**: Creates nodes for the main operations (`Queries`, `Mutations`, `Subscriptions`) by finding the correct types from the schema.

3. **AddDirectivesNode**: Adds directives and organizes their locations and arguments.

4. **AddTypesNode**: Adds all other types except the main operation types.

5. **Recursive Node Building**: Methods like `AddFieldNode`, `AddArgumentNode`, and `GetTypeDescription` handle nested fields, arguments, and types to display everything clearly.

### Additional Notes

- **Structure**: This should now provide a clear, error-free setup with organized methods for each top-level component.
- **Data Flow**: All original schema data remains intact, and each node’s recursive building handles child elements.

Let me know if this organization works for you or if further tweaks are needed!

I will start with the data models based on the original introspection schema structure, ensuring they contain the necessary properties for `queries`, `mutations`, `subscriptions`, `directives`, and `types`.

Here’s how we can set up the `Schema` and related classes, along with the revised `RadTreeViewPopulator` class to populate the `RadTreeView`.

### Step 1: Define the Correct Data Model for the Schema

Let’s define the data model based on the structure of GraphQL introspection JSON. Please replace your existing data models with the following:

```csharp
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Logistixs.ServerManager.Application
{
    public class Welcome
    {
        [JsonProperty("__schema")]
        public Schema Schema { get; set; }
    }

    public class Schema
    {
        [JsonProperty("queryType")]
        public TypeReference QueryType { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("mutationType")]
        public TypeReference MutationType { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("subscriptionType")]
        public TypeReference SubscriptionType { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("types")]
        public List<TypeElement> Types { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("directives")]
        public List<Directive> Directives { get; set; }
    }

    public class TypeReference
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }
    }

    public class TypeElement
    {
        [JsonProperty("kind")]
        public string Kind { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("fields")]
        public List<Field> Fields { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("inputFields")]
        public List<InputField> InputFields { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("interfaces")]
        public List<TypeReference> Interfaces { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("enumValues")]
        public List<EnumValue> EnumValues { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("possibleTypes")]
        public List<TypeReference> PossibleTypes { get; set; }
    }

    public class Field
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("args")]
        public List<Argument> Args { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("type")]
        public TypeReference Type { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("isDeprecated")]
        public bool IsDeprecated { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("deprecationReason")]
        public string DeprecationReason { get; set; }
    }

    public class Argument
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("type")]
        public TypeReference Type { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("defaultValue")]
        public string DefaultValue { get; set; }
    }

    public class Directive
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("locations")]
        public List<string> Locations { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("args")]
        public List<Argument> Args { get; set; }
    }

    public class EnumValue
    {
        [JsonProperty("name")]
        public string Name { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("description")]
        public string Description { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("isDeprecated")]
        public bool IsDeprecated { get; set; }

        [JsonProperty("deprecationReason")]
        public string DeprecationReason { get; set; }
    }

    public class InputField : Argument { }
}
```

### Step 2: Populate the `RadTreeView` Using the Correct Data Model

The `RadTreeViewPopulator` class will now correctly use this data model to add nodes for `Queries`, `Mutations`, `Subscriptions`, `Directives`, and `Types`.

```csharp
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Telerik.WinControls.UI;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using Logistixs.ServerManager.Application;

public class RadTreeViewPopulator
{
    private RadTreeView _treeView;

    public RadTreeViewPopulator(RadTreeView treeView)
    {
        _treeView = treeView;
    }

    public void PopulateTreeView(string jsonSchema)
    {
        var schemaData = JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<Welcome>(jsonSchema)?.Schema;
        if (schemaData == null) return;

        var rootNode = new RadTreeNode("GraphQL Schema");
        _treeView.Nodes.Add(rootNode);

        if (schemaData.QueryType != null)
            AddOperationNode(rootNode, "Queries", schemaData.QueryType.Name, schemaData.Types);

        if (schemaData.MutationType != null)
            AddOperationNode(rootNode, "Mutations", schemaData.MutationType.Name, schemaData.Types);

        if (schemaData.SubscriptionType != null)
            AddOperationNode(rootNode, "Subscriptions", schemaData.SubscriptionType.Name, schemaData.Types);

        AddDirectivesNode(rootNode, schemaData.Directives);
        AddTypesNode(rootNode, schemaData.Types);

        _treeView.ExpandAll();
    }

    private void AddOperationNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, string operationName, string typeName, List<TypeElement> types)
    {
        var operationNode = new RadTreeNode(operationName);
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(operationNode);

        var mainType = types.Find(t => t.Name == typeName && t.Kind == "OBJECT");
        if (mainType?.Fields != null)
        {
            foreach (var field in mainType.Fields)
            {
                AddFieldNode(operationNode, field);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddDirectivesNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, List<Directive> directives)
    {
        var directivesNode = new RadTreeNode("Directives");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(directivesNode);

        foreach (var directive in directives)
        {
            var directiveNode = new RadTreeNode(directive.Name);
            directivesNode.Nodes.Add(directiveNode);

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(directive.Description))
                directiveNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {directive.Description}"));

            var locationsNode = new RadTreeNode("Locations");
            directiveNode.Nodes.Add(locationsNode);
            foreach (var location in directive.Locations)
            {
                locationsNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode(location));
            }

            var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
            directiveNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
            foreach (var arg in directive.Args)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddTypesNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, List<TypeElement> types)
    {
        var typesNode = new RadTreeNode("Types");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(typesNode);

        foreach (var typeElement in types)
        {
            if (typeElement.Kind != "OBJECT" || 
                (typeElement.Name != "Query" && typeElement.Name != "Mutation" && typeElement.Name != "Subscription"))
            {
                AddTypeNode(typesNode, typeElement);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddTypeNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, TypeElement typeElement)
    {
        var typeNode = new RadTreeNode($"{typeElement.Name} ({typeElement.Kind})");
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(typeNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(typeElement.Description))
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {typeElement.Description}"));

        if (typeElement.Fields != null)
        {
            var fieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(fieldsNode);
            foreach (var field in typeElement.Fields)
            {
                AddFieldNode(fieldsNode, field);
            }
        }

        if (typeElement.InputFields != null)
        {
            var inputFieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Input Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(inputFieldsNode);
            foreach (var inputField in typeElement.InputFields)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(inputFieldsNode, inputField);
            }
        }

        if (typeElement.EnumValues != null)
        {

It looks like there were some syntax issues in the provided code, including missing braces and potentially incomplete methods. I'll correct these issues to ensure the code compiles correctly.

Here's the revised and complete `RadTreeViewPopulator` class with all syntax issues addressed:

```csharp
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Telerik.WinControls.UI;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using Logistixs.ServerManager.Application;

public class RadTreeViewPopulator
{
    private RadTreeView _treeView;

    public RadTreeViewPopulator(RadTreeView treeView)
    {
        _treeView = treeView;
    }

    public void PopulateTreeView(string jsonSchema)
    {
        var schemaData = JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<Welcome>(jsonSchema)?.Schema;
        if (schemaData == null) return;

        var rootNode = new RadTreeNode("GraphQL Schema");
        _treeView.Nodes.Add(rootNode);

        if (schemaData.QueryType != null)
            AddOperationNode(rootNode, "Queries", schemaData.QueryType.Name, schemaData.Types);

        if (schemaData.MutationType != null)
            AddOperationNode(rootNode, "Mutations", schemaData.MutationType.Name, schemaData.Types);

        if (schemaData.SubscriptionType != null)
            AddOperationNode(rootNode, "Subscriptions", schemaData.SubscriptionType.Name, schemaData.Types);

        AddDirectivesNode(rootNode, schemaData.Directives);
        AddTypesNode(rootNode, schemaData.Types);

        _treeView.ExpandAll();
    }

    private void AddOperationNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, string operationName, string typeName, List<TypeElement> types)
    {
        var operationNode = new RadTreeNode(operationName);
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(operationNode);

        var mainType = types.Find(t => t.Name == typeName && t.Kind == "OBJECT");
        if (mainType?.Fields != null)
        {
            foreach (var field in mainType.Fields)
            {
                AddFieldNode(operationNode, field);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddDirectivesNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, List<Directive> directives)
    {
        var directivesNode = new RadTreeNode("Directives");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(directivesNode);

        foreach (var directive in directives)
        {
            var directiveNode = new RadTreeNode(directive.Name);
            directivesNode.Nodes.Add(directiveNode);

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(directive.Description))
                directiveNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {directive.Description}"));

            var locationsNode = new RadTreeNode("Locations");
            directiveNode.Nodes.Add(locationsNode);
            foreach (var location in directive.Locations)
            {
                locationsNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode(location));
            }

            var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
            directiveNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
            foreach (var arg in directive.Args)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddTypesNode(RadTreeNode rootNode, List<TypeElement> types)
    {
        var typesNode = new RadTreeNode("Types");
        rootNode.Nodes.Add(typesNode);

        foreach (var typeElement in types)
        {
            if (typeElement.Kind != "OBJECT" || 
                (typeElement.Name != "Query" && typeElement.Name != "Mutation" && typeElement.Name != "Subscription"))
            {
                AddTypeNode(typesNode, typeElement);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddTypeNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, TypeElement typeElement)
    {
        var typeNode = new RadTreeNode($"{typeElement.Name} ({typeElement.Kind})");
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(typeNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(typeElement.Description))
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {typeElement.Description}"));

        if (typeElement.Fields != null)
        {
            var fieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(fieldsNode);
            foreach (var field in typeElement.Fields)
            {
                AddFieldNode(fieldsNode, field);
            }
        }

        if (typeElement.InputFields != null)
        {
            var inputFieldsNode = new RadTreeNode("Input Fields");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(inputFieldsNode);
            foreach (var inputField in typeElement.InputFields)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(inputFieldsNode, inputField);
            }
        }

        if (typeElement.EnumValues != null)
        {
            var enumValuesNode = new RadTreeNode("Enum Values");
            typeNode.Nodes.Add(enumValuesNode);
            foreach (var enumValue in typeElement.EnumValues)
            {
                var enumNode = new RadTreeNode($"{enumValue.Name} - {enumValue.Description}");
                enumValuesNode.Nodes.Add(enumNode);
            }
        }
    }

    private void AddFieldNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Field field)
    {
        var fieldNode = new RadTreeNode(field.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(fieldNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(field.Description))
            fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {field.Description}"));

        if (field.Args != null && field.Args.Count > 0)
        {
            var argsNode = new RadTreeNode("Arguments");
            fieldNode.Nodes.Add(argsNode);
            foreach (var arg in field.Args)
            {
                AddArgumentNode(argsNode, arg);
            }
        }

        fieldNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {field.Type?.Name}"));
    }

    private void AddArgumentNode(RadTreeNode parentNode, Argument arg)
    {
        var argNode = new RadTreeNode(arg.Name);
        parentNode.Nodes.Add(argNode);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(arg.Description))
            argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Description: {arg.Description}"));

        if (arg.Type != null)
            argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Type: {arg.Type.Name}"));

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(arg.DefaultValue))
            argNode.Nodes.Add(new RadTreeNode($"Default Value: {arg.DefaultValue}"));
    }
}
```

### Explanation of Fixes

1. **Missing Method Implementations**: I ensured that `AddArgumentNode` is defined and implemented, preventing `CS0103` errors.
2. **Correct `Type` Property Access**: Adjusted the `field.Type?.Name` and `arg.Type?.Name` properties to display the type’s name correctly.
3. **Fixed Syntax Errors**: Corrected any open braces `{` or open interpolated strings `${` that were left incomplete.

With this implementation:
- Each section (Queries, Mutations, Subscriptions, Directives, and Types) is created under a separate node.
- Each node is populated recursively with fields, arguments, and subtypes as per the structure of the introspection JSON. 

This should compile without errors and align with your introspection data structure. Let me know if any adjustments are needed for further alignment with your schema.

using GraphQL.Client.Http;
using GraphQL.Client.Serializer.Newtonsoft;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using Newtonsoft.Json.Linq;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Logistixs.ServerManager.Application
{
    public class GraphQLService
    {
        private readonly GraphQLHttpClient _client;

        public GraphQLService(string endpointUrl, string token)
        {
            _client = new GraphQLHttpClient(endpointUrl, new NewtonsoftJsonSerializer());
            _client.HttpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new System.Net.Http.Headers.AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token);
        }

        public async Task GetSchema()
        {
            // Define the introspection query
            var IntrospectionQuery = @"
                       query IntrospectionQuery {
                        __schema {
                            queryType {
                                name
                            }
                            mutationType {
                                name
                            }
                            subscriptionType {
                                name
                            }
                            types {
                                ...FullType
                            }
                            directives {
                                name
                                description
                                locations
                                args {
                                    ...InputValue
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    fragment FullType on __Type {
                        kind
                        name
                        description
                        fields(includeDeprecated: true) {
                            name
                            description
                            args {
                                ...InputValue
                            }
                            type {
                                ...TypeRef
                            }
                            isDeprecated
                            deprecationReason
                        }
                        inputFields {
                            ...InputValue
                        }
                        interfaces {
                            ...TypeRef
                        }
                        enumValues(includeDeprecated: true) {
                            name
                            description
                            isDeprecated
                            deprecationReason
                        }
                        possibleTypes {
                            ...TypeRef
                        }
                    }
                    fragment InputValue on __InputValue {
                        name
                        description
                        type {
                            ...TypeRef
                        }
                        defaultValue
                    }
                    fragment TypeRef on __Type {
                        kind
                        name
                        ofType {
                            kind
                            name
                            ofType {
                                kind
                                name
                                ofType {
                                    kind
                                    name
                                    ofType {
                                        kind
                                        name
                                        ofType {
                                            kind
                                            name
                                            ofType {
                                                kind
                                                name
                                                ofType {
                                                    kind
                                                    name
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }";

            // Step 1: Send the introspection query and get the response
            var request = new GraphQLRequest { Query = IntrospectionQuery };
            var response = await _client.SendQueryAsync<JObject>(request);

            // Step 2: Convert the response to a JSON string for easier inspection
            string jsonString = response.Data.ToString();
            Debug.WriteLine("Raw JSON Response:");
            Debug.WriteLine(jsonString); // Output the JSON string to verify structure

            // Step 3: Attempt to deserialize the entire JSON response into the `Welcome` class
            try
            {
                // Deserialize directly into Welcome object
                var welcome = JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<Welcome>(jsonString);

                // Step 4: Check if the schema is populated
                if (welcome?.Schema != null)
                {
                    Debug.WriteLine("Deserialization succeeded. Schema is populated.");
                    Debug.WriteLine(JsonConvert.SerializeObject(welcome.Schema, Formatting.Indented));
                }
                else
                {
                    Debug.WriteLine("Deserialization failed: Schema or Data is null.");
                }
            }
            catch (JsonException ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Deserialization error: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }
    }
}
